## Preflight interface scan

| Phases | Shared interface | Check and disposition |
|---|---|---|
| A/B | worker health, config/generation/boot binding | One shared schema plus authenticated host bridge; cached health cannot grant runtime authority. |
| A/C | secrets, config and preserved identity | Zeroization must preserve duplicate-key semantics; migration backups private and stopped-state only. |
| B/C | fleet/guard/client ownership and selected node | Stable exit/node identity and explicit mode shared; direct never automatic fallback. |
| B/D | host placement and service-manager cgroups | Native verified hierarchy-specific adapters; no path-only or broad capability authorization. |
| C/D | migration/release activation and rollback | REAL release directory and compatible stopped config/state; no symlink-only rollback. |
| D/D | fault testing and performance samples | Same exact final bytes; no reduced security cadence or coverage; real24h elapsed required. |
| Task1 self | implementation versus acceptance | All9areas remain scope; unavailable local environments are NOT_RUN, never synthetic certification. |

Ruling: retain the existing approved architecture and use this request as authorization to implement the nine-item design — the user explicitly requested all items after the concrete table; cost if wrong is reviewable code rework, not production activation.
Ruling: one implementer sequentially owns the connected scope, per the user's request, and reports checkpoints before independent verification — cost is longer context management; durable report and followups mitigate it.
Ruling: production activation/publication are excluded; privileged acceptance uses isolated local environments controlled by root — existing contract preserves runtime and identity; cost is unreleased code until full evidence exists.

Reviewer caught a real historical sequence-transfer defect before native execution: original Query accepts child-reported sequences then parent owned_down readback advances route sequence. Using the immutable child report for the new Move exact comparison would refuse valid custody. Query now separately records accepted_sequences only after successful original parent readback/public comparison/current receipt and original clock checks. Move uses that private stored state; child report and original timer/history remain immutable, equality/empty-queue checks stay strict. The actual movement fixture requires original Query query_sequences show parent_route > child_route and unchanged generic sequence, tied to the same original native receipt/ACK and original socket registrations. Native execution of that regression remains NOT_RUN until Root297+; no fabricated Completed or lowered sequence condition is used.
